# REV-4 test importance scores: current suite

Baseline `2e87ab15ffee382b1685bf38f233eca3d76fe72e`. All 1,203 current declarations: 505 Go and 698 TypeScript. Current baseline source lines and exact names identify each row, including duplicate titles in separate describe blocks. The previous 50 deletions are outside this current-suite review.

Scores follow [SCORE_REVIEW.md](SCORE_REVIEW.md): 0 = definitely expendable with named surviving equivalent protection; 10 = critical integrity protection that must survive. Scores are ordinal judgments, not mutation measurements. Focused = directly checked body/overlap or critical contract. Screened = provisional score assisted by source-signal rules over declaration bodies, targets and assertions; it is not a completed pairwise redundancy audit. Each parameterized declaration is rated as a combined contract.

No deletions are performed by this scoring pass. Low scores do not authorize deleting an entire band or the named counterpart at the same time.
